# META
~~~ini
description=Reference FeatureValues retain resolved operands and sequences warn on structural values that do not conform but may still overlap, and reject a behavior bound to an item (Performances::Performance is disjoint from Objects::Object) without changing lexical shadowing; KerML 8.4.4.11 feature-value binding semantics, 7.4.6.1 overlap
specification=OMG KerML 1.0 (formal/26-03-01), 8.4.4.11
specification_url=https://www.omg.org/spec/KerML/1.0/PDF
type=file
libraries=standard
require_complete_publication=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package ReferenceValues {
    item def Value;
    item def SpecialValue :> Value;
    item def Other;
    item a : Value;
    item b : SpecialValue;
    item wrong : Other;
    item def Holder { ref item values : Value [0..*]; }
    item valid : Holder { ref item :>> values = a; }
    item sequence : Holder { ref item :>> values = (a, b); }
    item invalid : Holder { ref item :>> values = wrong; }
    item mixed : Holder { ref item :>> values = (a, wrong); }
    verification def Check { ref item value : Value; }
    verification shadow : Check { ref item :>> value = shadow; }
    verification qualified : Check { ref item :>> value = ReferenceValues::a; }
    part def Container { item nested : SpecialValue; }
    part container : Container;
    item dotted : Holder { ref item :>> values = container.nested; }
}
~~~
